- **retrospective-writer**: Use this agent when the user needs trading retrospective documents generated from D:/trade data for 复盘 (post-trade review), 周报 (weekly summary), or 月报 (monthly summary). Typical triggers include 复盘, 平仓了, 这一单结束了, 帮我写复盘, 周报, 这周怎么样, 本周总结, 月报, 本月总结, and 找一下之前类似的复盘/行情 (similar-review retrieval from the local BM25 index). See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. &lt;example&gt; Context: User just closed an ARB long position and wants the review written up. user: "复盘 ARB 这一单" assistant: "I'll use the retrospective-writer agent to generate and archive the review." &lt;commentary&gt; Post-trad… ([userzbb/trade-agents](https://github.com/userzbb/trade-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/retrospective-writer.md) · ★1)

Не пишет код и не пишет в память сам — выдаёт JSON, который обвязка кладёт в память. Использовать после FAIL-вердикта или закрытия задачи. - **review-gate**: Validates that code fixes correctly address their review findings. Used by the address-findings orchestrator after fix commits are integrated. Receives finding IDs and a VCS diff, returns PASS/FAIL per finding. ([fzymgc-house/fzymgc-house-skills](https://github.com/fzymgc-house/fzymgc-house-skills/blob/HEAD/dev-flow/agents/review-gate.md))
- **review-gate**: Independent reviewer for Knitty code changes. Audits the current branch's diff against acceptance criteria, ADRs, crate-boundary rules, Rust idioms, and test coverage. Returns findings as Must-fix / Should-fix / Nit. Invoked from the /review-gate skill. ([knitty-io/knitty](https://github.com/knitty-io/knitty/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-gate.md))
- **review-gates**: Таблица, по которой изменённые файлы превращаются в список проверок. Применяет её команда `/review-pr` (шаг 4) к дифу PR и передаёт список включённых гейтов скиллу ревью. По ней же отвечают на второй вопрос — устарела ли запись мутационного прогона (ниже, «Изменения, которые влияют на прогон мутаций»); этот вопрос задают и автор, и ревьюер, и диф там другой. Поэтому таблица лежит отдельным докумен ([yuldashevsardor/telegram-bot](https://github.com/yuldashevsardor/telegram-bot/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/review-gates.md) · ★2)
- **review-gates**: Significant work carries review gates: a review through one lens, run by the matching gate agent in `.claude/agents/` (`gate-game-design`, `gate-tech-architecture`, `gate-product-vision`). The session performing a workflow owns firing the right gates; Mark is never the one who has to remember. Fire multiple gates concurrently when more than one applies. ([niftymonkey/the-cabinet](https://github.com/niftymonkey/the-cabinet/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/review-gates.md))

Nothing from the draft was dropped. ([niftymonkey/the-cabinet](https://github.com/niftymonkey/the-cabinet/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/review-gates-provenance.md))
- **review-gatherer**: Collect PR review comments, review threads and inline feedback, then return them deduplicated, grouped by file, with resolution status and permalinks. ([hoblin/claude-ruby-marketplace](https://github.com/hoblin/claude-ruby-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/rpi/agents/review-generic.md) · ★37)

- **review-glossary**: Reviews a PR for terminology drift against docs/glossary.md. Use as part of /code-review or as a sub-agent in /implement review waves; also invoked by create-issue before issue creation. Flags load-bearing terms that diverge from the glossary and PRs that introduce a new domain term without adding an entry. Does not write to the glossary — the writing agent adds entries when addressing findings. ([khmelevartem/tether](https://github.com/khmelevartem/tether/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-glossary.md) · ★1)
- **review-google-style**: Specialist code reviewer for Google STYLE-GUIDE compliance — JS/TS style guide and Python pyguide rules, plus eng-practices observations that lint cannot mechanically detect (small CL discipline, CL description shape, doc shape, naming intent). Cites RUBRIC.yaml rule IDs and Google source anchors. Returns a structured verdict the panel chair synthesizes. ([drumfiend21/claude-tdd-pro](https://github.com/drumfiend21/claude-tdd-pro/blob/HEAD/agents/review-google-style.md))
- **review-governor**: 笛语审查总控 Agent：接收审查请求 → 自动路由场景 → 锁定真源 → 按需加载参考 → 执行审查 → 输出结构化结论。独立上下文运行，不污染主对话。 ([andyan77/diyu-agent-legacy-20260711T234248Z](https://github.com/andyan77/diyu-agent-legacy-20260711T234248Z/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-governor.md))
- **review-grader**: Internal subagent. Invoke only when explicitly dispatched by an orchestrator skill. ([gautam-achieveai/ClaudePlugins](https://github.com/gautam-achieveai/ClaudePlugins/blob/HEAD/code-reviewer/agents/review-grader.md) · ★3)
- **review-grounder**: Grounding phase agent for the code review pipeline. Reads the diff, maps the integration surface, builds the three-layer model, and stores structured grounding context via MCP. Use when spawning the grounding teammate. ([GaijinEntertainment/yatai](https://github.com/GaijinEntertainment/yatai/blob/HEAD/claude/kensai/agents/review-grounder.md))
- **review-gui-ux**: GUI/UX review lens for McRPG PRs — inventory slot ergonomics, navigation, click-hint and localization conventions, player feedback, palette usage. ([DiamondDagger590/McRPG](https://github.com/DiamondDagger590/McRPG/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-gui-ux.md) · ★37)
- **review-guidelines**: You are an expert code reviewer specializing in project conventions, consistency, and standards compliance. Your primary goal is ensuring new code matches the project's established patterns with high precision - minimize false positives. - **review-guidelines**: Guidelines reviewer for code review. Checks compliance with project rules, conventions, and established patterns. Requires diff output, diff stat output, and PR/MR description (if any) as context. ([ttakanawa/dotfiles](https://github.com/ttakanawa/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-guidelines.md))
- **review-guides**: Reviews a PR for conformance to CLAUDE.md and docs/engineering/*. Use as part of /code-review orchestration. Flags violations of project conventions, idioms, DI rules, layering, comment style, commit naming. Does not check correctness or platform parity. ([khmelevartem/tether](https://github.com/khmelevartem/tether/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-guides.md) · ★1)
- **review-helper**: Helper agent for the creview skills (start / triage / respond / resolve / rounds), responsible for aggregation, compilation, analysis, and format & build verification. Assists the destination project's specialist agents and sticks to mechanical, procedural, template-driven work. ([OPENSPHERE-Inc/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/OPENSPHERE-Inc/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/creview/agents/review-helper.md))
- **review-helper**: creview スキル群（start / triage / respond / resolve / rounds）の助手エージェント。集約・編纂・解析・フォーマット&ビルド検証を担う。移譲先プロジェクトの専門家エージェントを補佐し、機械的・手続的・テンプレート駆動の作業に徹する。 ([OPENSPHERE-Inc/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/OPENSPHERE-Inc/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/src/creview/agents/review-helper.md))
- **review-history**: Check git blame and historical context of modified code ([prodelt/elt-harness](https://github.com/prodelt/elt-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/review-history.md) · ★1)
- **review-history**: /sdd:review のエージェント B。チームが過去の PR で受けた指摘の傾向 (指摘データ) を Phase の差分に当て、同じ指摘が付きそうな箇所を先に示す。読み取りだけで code は編集しない。 ([DaichiHoshina/spec-ramo](https://github.com/DaichiHoshina/spec-ramo/blob/HEAD/agents/review-history.md))
- **review-hygiene**: Reviews code hygiene — dead code, unused exports, commented-out blocks, debug artifacts, stale TODOs, copy-paste duplication ([neolao/claude-plugin-vibe](https://github.com/neolao/claude-plugin-vibe/blob/HEAD/agents/review-hygiene.md))
- **review-idea-pack**: Fresh-context reviewer that gates docs/idea/idea-pack.md before the human approves it. Route here when the Idea Pack has been drafted (by the idea-pack skill or by hand) and someone asks to review, check, sanity-check, or sign off the Idea Pack, or before moving on to the PRD. Do NOT route here to write or fix the Idea Pack; this agent only judges. ([jiffi-co/claude-plugins](https://github.com/jiffi-co/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/builder-kit/agents/review-idea-pack.md))
- **review-impact**: Reviews code changes for dropped guards and removed invariants (deleted lines) and for caller-side breakage from changed function signatures. ([synesenom/ran](https://github.com/synesenom/ran/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-impact.md) · ★15)
- **review-impact**: Invoked during the build/push review step. Checks for breaking changes to public interfaces, unexpected side effects, performance regressions, and database query changes. ([joshevensen/hexbyte-plugins](https://github.com/joshevensen/hexbyte-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/orc/agents/review-impact.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **review-impact-evaluator**: Scores the IMPACT of acting on a PR review — whether the agent's follow-up resolved the flagged issues and left the build green. Adjudicates resolution semantically (so multi-file and non-contiguous refactors count), uses match.py only as a cross-check, and hands all math to score.py. Run after review-quality-evaluator for the same run. Writes to evals/ only through score.py. ([Haadhi76/workflow](https://github.com/Haadhi76/workflow/blob/HEAD/Workflow_Evals/pr-review-evals/pr-review-evals/.claude/agents/review-impact-evaluator.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-impl**: Conformance gate. Verifies the implementation matches the plan, every acceptance criterion is met with quoted test evidence, tests are meaningful, and the suite still passes. Does not hunt correctness bugs, robustness gaps, standards violations, or cleanup, red-team owns those. Use after implementation chunks are complete, before final commit. ([KashZod/devloop](https://github.com/KashZod/devloop/blob/HEAD/agents/review-impl.md) · ★2)
- **review-implementation**: Reviews code changes after implementation for correctness, patterns compliance, and test coverage. Acts as a quality gate — returns PASS or REJECTED with specific issues. ([AbsolutSystems/absolutpowers](https://github.com/AbsolutSystems/absolutpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/review-implementation.md) · ★1)
- **review-implementation**: Read-only code reviewer - checks whether the diff achieves its stated goal, is wired up, and stays inside what was asked for. A /dev:review agent. ([Villhard/nxs](https://github.com/Villhard/nxs/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/agents/review-implementation.md) · ★1)
- **review-implementer**: Implements a PR’s review action list, commits in small logical steps, and resolves GitHub review threads with “Done” replies when finished. Use when review-actions.md exists for a PR. ([prisma/orm](https://github.com/prisma/orm/blob/HEAD/skills-contrib/review-implement-phase/agents/review-implementer.md) · ★48k · 2 copies)
- **review-infra**: Reviews container, CI and server config changes — validation, exposed ports, secrets, privileges, limits, pinned versions, rollback — and records a review JSON. ([yolol312/goodboy](https://github.com/yolol312/goodboy/blob/HEAD/agents/review-infra.md))
- **review-ingest**: Fresh-context reviewer that gates the artifacts derived by the ingest skill before anyone relies on the auto-accepted facts. Route here after ingest has scanned an existing prototype or repo and written derived artifacts, decisions, and the Prototype Bible, and someone asks to review, sanity-check, or sign off the ingestion before moving on to architect. Do NOT route here to write or fix the derived artifacts; this agent only judges. ([jiffi-co/claude-plugins](https://github.com/jiffi-co/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/builder-kit/agents/review-ingest.md))
- **review-intake**: Process external review feedback — read ALL items first, verify claims against codebase, then implement in priority order. Use when receiving PR comments or code review. ([Rune-kit/rune](https://github.com/Rune-kit/rune/blob/HEAD/agents/review-intake.md) · ★87 · 2 copies)
- **review-intake**: Cold per-claim premise judge for incoming review findings. Dispatched by the gateway before any implementer sees a judgment-grade review finding. Verifies each claim against the code it cites and returns confirmed / falsified / needs-evidence per claim with the evidence line, plus a coverage line the gateway bounces on mismatch. Text only — writes nothing; falsified claims stop at the gateway. ([RockyHong/super-bootstrap](https://github.com/RockyHong/super-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/plugins/super-bootstrap/agents/review-intake.md) · ★3)
- **review-integrator**: Applies a reviewer's findings to the target artifact with reasoning annotations; escalates disagreements instead of skipping them. ([dbc-oduffy/coordinator-claude](https://github.com/dbc-oduffy/coordinator-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/review-integrator.md) · ★5)
- **review-intent**: Independent read-only intent reviewer. Compares the diff against stated acceptance criteria and non-goals to find unmet requirements, violated non-goals, undeclared behavior, and missing required edge cases. Judges "is this what was asked", not "is this elegant". Use when an intent file or PR description exists. ([Jiusi-pys/agent-plugins](https://github.com/Jiusi-pys/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/multi-review/agents/review-intent.md))
- **review-intent-gate**: dialog-harness PR レビューの Phase 2-a 意図ゲート判定ワーカー（安価）。claude-review.yml の OC が Haiku ティアで起動する。PR 本文が実質的な diff の意図・トレードオフを述べているかを diff 単位で判定し JSON を返す。意図不明な diff は後段で修正提案を停止する根拠になる。 ([samejima-ai/dialog-harness](https://github.com/samejima-ai/dialog-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-intent-gate.md) · ★1)
- **review-internal**: Code review specialist for own-repo PRs — correctness, security (OWASP Top 10), and pattern adherence. ([Manolii-org/ai-starter-pack](https://github.com/Manolii-org/ai-starter-pack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-internal.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-invariant-ecs**: Audits a PR diff against ECS correctness invariants in full file context and returns a review fragment with file:line citations. Use from review-pr when a PR touches systems, components, or entity-lifecycle code. ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-invariant-ecs.md) · ★8)
- **review-invariant-render**: Audits a PR diff that touches engine/render/, engine/prefabs/irreden/render/, or shaders against render-pipeline invariants (CPU/GPU struct layout, binding points, dispatch grids, lighting stages, GPU lifetime) and returns a review fragment with file:line citations. Use from review-pr whenever a PR touches render code or shader source. ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-invariant-render.md) · ★8)
- **review-invariants**: Repo-specific review rules for `ziw-code-review` (the org skill's bug taxonomy is generic; these are the traps that have actually bitten this codebase). Load this alongside the skill's checklist when reviewing Trace Flow diffs or PRs. ([zaks-io/trace-flow](https://github.com/zaks-io/trace-flow/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/review-invariants.md))
- **review-judge**: This role is mandatory only for schema v2 strict and the legacy code-review pipeline. Standard uses one independent general review; unresolved/conflicting findings needing adjudication must upgrade to strict before dispatching this role. Light has no judge. All adjudication requirements below apply to strict/legacy. Mode B must inspect the actual current revision and each finding's disposition; pr ([Fize/shipmate](https://github.com/Fize/shipmate/blob/HEAD/workflow-orchestrator/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judge**: Synthesizes verified verdicts, deterministic-gate state and risk tier into a single gate decision (PASS / PASS_WITH_NOTES / HUMAN_REQUIRED / BLOCK / REJECT_INTAKE) plus a concise human report. Only verified findings may block. Use as the final step of the multi-review pipeline. ([Jiusi-pys/agent-plugins](https://github.com/Jiusi-pys/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/multi-review/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judge**: Settles a conflict between the Standards axis and the Spec axis of a code review, where one says the code must be a certain way and the other says that same thing is wrong. Invoked by groundwork:code-review only for real contradictions, never as a routine pass over every finding. ([mauriciovieira/groundwork](https://github.com/mauriciovieira/groundwork/blob/HEAD/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judge**: Independent reviewer-readonly-v1 second pass. Reads changed files fresh from the main-thread REVIEW PACKET v1, checks cross-cutting gaps and panel quality, and performs no execution or mutation. ([MKITConsulting/zensu-claude-code](https://github.com/MKITConsulting/zensu-claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judge**: Fresh-context judge for ONE dimension of a project review. Invoked only by the project-review skill, which supplies the evidence directory and the criteria file. Never delegate to this agent for any other task. ([vdaluz/claude-skills](https://github.com/vdaluz/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/general-workflow/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judgment**: dialog-harness PR レビュー Council の判定エージェント。claude-review.yml の OC が Phase 3-c で起動する。3 ペルソナ出力 + final_weights + conflict_type を受け取り、weighted_score = Σ(weight×confidence) で recommended を決定し、output-format.md §4 の判定 JSON のみ返す。final_decision は常に null。 ([samejima-ai/dialog-harness](https://github.com/samejima-ai/dialog-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-judgment.md) · ★1)
- **review-judgment**: The one LLM reviewer in phase 6. Judges intent, cross-file logic, architectural fit, and whether a disclosed shortcut is real. Everything mechanical is already enforced by `bun run doctor`. ([Eduardo-Barreto/web-project-template](https://github.com/Eduardo-Barreto/web-project-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-judgment.md))
- **review-karpathy**: Andrej Karpathy의 LLM 코딩 원칙 기반 리뷰어. "모델이 확인 없이 잘못된 가정을 하고, 코드를 과도하게 복잡하게 만든다"는 관찰을 바탕으로 단순성·수술적 변경·목표 지향 실행을 중심으로 코드를 리뷰합니다. 코드 작성 직후 선제적으로 사용하십시오. ([juyohan/genie-plugin](https://github.com/juyohan/genie-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/genie/agents/review-karpathy.md) · ★1)
- **review-lad-wrapper**: Review-LAD specialist agent for the BMAD Agent Development Automator. Composes the upstream `lad_mcp_server` MCP `code_review` tool (Shelpuk-AI-Technology-Consulting/lad_mcp_server, version_floor `bb47e9e`, OpenRouter-backed dual-reviewer parallelism per ADR-008) and returns a sensor-not-advisor envelope. Dispatched by the orchestrator skill via the Claude Code Task tool per ADR-004; the agent definition file is read AS DATA by the dispatch substrate (never imported as code). - **review-panel**: A single reviewer seat on the pr-review-loop panel (Reviewer A Architecture, B Correctness, C Performance/Quality, or E Seams & Blast Radius — the caller's prompt says which). Reads a diff plus whatever source the review-core lenses require, and returns findings as JSON. Spawn one per persona, in parallel. Use this instead of general-purpose for every reviewer seat: a code reviewer only ever needs Read/Grep/Glob/Bash, and a general-purpose agent re-sends ~100 unused MCP tool schemas on every turn — measured at ~5.4k tokens per turn, the single largest cost in the pipeline. Returns qa-report.json with verdict: approved or needs_revision. Read-only — never modifies the PRD itself. Read-only; cites file:line + severity. Trigger: "Use review-qa-engineer to audit [scope]". Part of the principal-review team — challenge the others; defer pixels/contract/runtime mechanics to them. ([akin-oz/sorrel](https://github.com/akin-oz/sorrel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-qa-engineer.md))
